        Idea:
Add audio-reactive effects to the video editor. Effects should be able to respond to audio data (frequency bins, amplitude, beat detection) from a blended mix of all audio tracks in the timeline. This must integrate with the existing effects infrastructure: the DynamicEffectRegistry, the compileEffect.tsx runtime compilation (inject audio hooks as new globals alongside useCurrentFrame/interpolate/etc), the effectPromptTemplate.ts (so AI-generated effects can use audio data), and the ParameterControls system (new audio-binding parameter type). Key integration points: (1) Audio analysis layer that extracts frequency/amplitude/beat data from a mixed-down version of all timeline audio tracks, synchronized to Remotion's frame clock. (2) A useAudioReactive() hook (or similar) injected into compiled custom effects via the new Function() call in compileEffect.tsx. (3) Updated effectPromptTemplate.ts documenting the new audio globals. (4) The continuous effect category is the primary target but entrance/exit effects should also be able to use audio data. (5) Preview support in EffectCreatorPanel.tsx. The existing effect wrapping pipeline (wrapWithEffect/wrapWithClipEffects in effects/index.tsx -> VisualClip.tsx -> EffectLayerSequence.tsx) should remain unchanged - audio data flows through the hook, not through props.

The plan TEXT says `cancelRender()` on unmount \u2014 this is WRONG. The gate warnings override: use `continueRender(handle)` on unmount. cancelRender throws and aborts the render.\n2. The plan TEXT says `delayRender()` on mount \u2014 ambiguous. The gate clarifies: acquire via `useState(() => delayRender(...))` for synchronous blocking, NOT in useEffect.\n3. The plan TEXT says `interpolate(...)` in useAudioParam \u2014 unnecessary. Use `value * (max - min) + min`.\n4. The plan TEXT says \"dual-value Slider or two number inputs\" \u2014 the Slider is scalar-only. Use two NumberInput controls.\n\n**Execution order:** T1 is the foundation (types). T2+T3+T4 are sequential (provider \u2192 hook \u2192 injection). T5, T6 can run in parallel with T2-T4 since they only depend on T1. T7, T8 depend on T2 (need the provider). T9 is the final validation gate after everything else.\n\n**FFT implementation:** Use a minimal inline radix-2 FFT. At 44.1kHz/30fps, each frame window is ~1470 samples \u2192 use 1024-point FFT (512 unique bins, ~43Hz resolution). If the FFT + analysis code pushes AudioAnalysisProvider past ~300 lines, extract FFT to a utility file like `src/tools/video-editor/compositions/fft.ts`.\n\n**Testing gap acknowledged:** The edge function index.test.ts mocks buildGenerateEffectMessages, so it can't validate templates.ts edits. - [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the render-gating cleanup plan against remotion's runtime exports in `node_modules/remotion/dist/cjs/cancel-render.js:46-70`. `cancelrender()` stores an error and throws to abort the render; it is not a benign cleanup for an abandoned `delayrender` handle, so step 2's instruction to call `cancelrender(handle)` on unmount is technically wrong and would abort preview or export with an error. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked how remotion itself acquires delay handles in `node_modules/remotion/dist/cjs/iframe.js:8-18`. remotion's own components create the handle synchronously with `usestate(() => delayrender(...))`; the plan says 'calls delayrender() on mount', which reads like an effect-time action and leaves the initial-frame blocking behavior underspecified compared with the actual library pattern. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app

        Idea:
Add audio-reactive effects to the video editor. Effects should be able to respond to audio data (frequency bins, amplitude, beat detection) from a blended mix of all audio tracks in the timeline. This must integrate with the existing effects infrastructure: the DynamicEffectRegistry, the compileEffect.tsx runtime compilation (inject audio hooks as new globals alongside useCurrentFrame/interpolate/etc), the effectPromptTemplate.ts (so AI-generated effects can use audio data), and the ParameterControls system (new audio-binding parameter type). Key integration points: (1) Audio analysis layer that extracts frequency/amplitude/beat data from a mixed-down version of all timeline audio tracks, synchronized to Remotion's frame clock. (2) A useAudioReactive() hook (or similar) injected into compiled custom effects via the new Function() call in compileEffect.tsx. (3) Updated effectPromptTemplate.ts documenting the new audio globals. (4) The continuous effect category is the primary target but entrance/exit effects should also be able to use audio data. (5) Preview support in EffectCreatorPanel.tsx. The existing effect wrapping pipeline (wrapWithEffect/wrapWithClipEffects in effects/index.tsx -> VisualClip.tsx -> EffectLayerSequence.tsx) should remain unchanged - audio data flows through the hook, not through props.

        Project directory:

        Idea:
Add audio-reactive effects to the video editor. Effects should be able to respond to audio data (frequency bins, amplitude, beat detection) from a blended mix of all audio tracks in the timeline. This must integrate with the existing effects infrastructure: the DynamicEffectRegistry, the compileEffect.tsx runtime compilation (inject audio hooks as new globals alongside useCurrentFrame/interpolate/etc), the effectPromptTemplate.ts (so AI-generated effects can use audio data), and the ParameterControls system (new audio-binding parameter type). Key integration points: (1) Audio analysis layer that extracts frequency/amplitude/beat data from a mixed-down version of all timeline audio tracks, synchronized to Remotion's frame clock. (2) A useAudioReactive() hook (or similar) injected into compiled custom effects via the new Function() call in compileEffect.tsx. (3) Updated effectPromptTemplate.ts documenting the new audio globals. (4) The continuous effect category is the primary target but entrance/exit effects should also be able to use audio data. (5) Preview support in EffectCreatorPanel.tsx. The existing effect wrapping pipeline (wrapWithEffect/wrapWithClipEffects in effects/index.tsx -> VisualClip.tsx -> EffectLayerSequence.tsx) should remain unchanged - audio data flows through the hook, not through props.

        Batch framing:
        - Execute batch 3 of 5.
        - Actionable task IDs for this batch: ['T3', 'T7', 'T8']

The plan TEXT says `delayRender()` on mount \u2014 ambiguous. The gate clarifies: acquire via `useState(() => delayRender(...))` for synchronous blocking, NOT in useEffect.\n3. The plan TEXT says `interpolate(...)` in useAudioParam \u2014 unnecessary. Use `value * (max - min) + min`.\n4. The plan TEXT says \"dual-value Slider or two number inputs\" \u2014 the Slider is scalar-only. Use two NumberInput controls.\n\n**Execution order:** T1 is the foundation (types). T2+T3+T4 are sequential (provider \u2192 hook \u2192 injection). T5, T6 can run in parallel with T2-T4 since they only depend on T1. T7, T8 depend on T2 (need the provider). T9 is the final validation gate after everything else.\n\n**FFT implementation:** Use a minimal inline radix-2 FFT. At 44.1kHz/30fps, each frame window is ~1470 samples \u2192 use 1024-point FFT (512 unique bins, ~43Hz resolution). If the FFT + analysis code pushes AudioAnalysisProvider past ~300 lines, extract FFT to a utility file like `src/tools/video-editor/compositions/fft.ts`.\n\n**Testing gap acknowledged:** The edge function index.test.ts mocks buildGenerateEffectMessages, so it can't validate templates.ts edits. T9 includes adding a direct templates.ts smoke test to close this gap.",

        Idea:
Add audio-reactive effects to the video editor. Effects should be able to respond to audio data (frequency bins, amplitude, beat detection) from a blended mix of all audio tracks in the timeline. This must integrate with the existing effects infrastructure: the DynamicEffectRegistry, the compileEffect.tsx runtime compilation (inject audio hooks as new globals alongside useCurrentFrame/interpolate/etc), the effectPromptTemplate.ts (so AI-generated effects can use audio data), and the ParameterControls system (new audio-binding parameter type). Key integration points: (1) Audio analysis layer that extracts frequency/amplitude/beat data from a mixed-down version of all timeline audio tracks, synchronized to Remotion's frame clock. (2) A useAudioReactive() hook (or similar) injected into compiled custom effects via the new Function() call in compileEffect.tsx. (3) Updated effectPromptTemplate.ts documenting the new audio globals. (4) The continuous effect category is the primary target but entrance/exit effects should also be able to use audio data. (5) Preview support in EffectCreatorPanel.tsx. The existing effect wrapping pipeline (wrapWithEffect/wrapWithClipEffects in effects/index.tsx -> VisualClip.tsx -> EffectLayerSequence.tsx) should remain unchanged - audio data flows through the hook, not through props.

The plan TEXT says `delayRender()` on mount \u2014 ambiguous. The gate clarifies: acquire via `useState(() => delayRender(...))` for synchronous blocking, NOT in useEffect.\n3. The plan TEXT says `interpolate(...)` in useAudioParam \u2014 unnecessary. Use `value * (max - min) + min`.\n4. The plan TEXT says \"dual-value Slider or two number inputs\" \u2014 the Slider is scalar-only. Use two NumberInput controls.\n\n**Execution order:** T1 is the foundation (types). T2+T3+T4 are sequential (provider \u2192 hook \u2192 injection). T5, T6 can run in parallel with T2-T4 since they only depend on T1. T7, T8 depend on T2 (need the provider). T9 is the final validation gate after everything else.\n\n**FFT implementation:** Use a minimal inline radix-2 FFT. At 44.1kHz/30fps, each frame window is ~1470 samples \u2192 use 1024-point FFT (512 unique bins, ~43Hz resolution). If the FFT + analysis code pushes AudioAnalysisProvider past ~300 lines, extract FFT to a utility file like `src/tools/video-editor/compositions/fft.ts`.\n\n**Testing gap acknowledged:** The edge function index.test.ts mocks buildGenerateEffectMessages, so it can't validate templates.ts edits. T9 includes adding a direct templates.ts smoke test to close this gap.",

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app

        Idea:
Add audio-reactive effects to the video editor. Effects should be able to respond to audio data (frequency bins, amplitude, beat detection) from a blended mix of all audio tracks in the timeline. This must integrate with the existing effects infrastructure: the DynamicEffectRegistry, the compileEffect.tsx runtime compilation (inject audio hooks as new globals alongside useCurrentFrame/interpolate/etc), the effectPromptTemplate.ts (so AI-generated effects can use audio data), and the ParameterControls system (new audio-binding parameter type). Key integration points: (1) Audio analysis layer that extracts frequency/amplitude/beat data from a mixed-down version of all timeline audio tracks, synchronized to Remotion's frame clock. (2) A useAudioReactive() hook (or similar) injected into compiled custom effects via the new Function() call in compileEffect.tsx. (3) Updated effectPromptTemplate.ts documenting the new audio globals. (4) The continuous effect category is the primary target but entrance/exit effects should also be able to use audio data. (5) Preview support in EffectCreatorPanel.tsx. The existing effect wrapping pipeline (wrapWithEffect/wrapWithClipEffects in effects/index.tsx -> VisualClip.tsx -> EffectLayerSequence.tsx) should remain unchanged - audio data flows through the hook, not through props.

  "meta_commentary": "This is a large feature spanning 13+ files across frontend and edge functions. The plan is well-reviewed (2 critique iterations, gate passed) with several critical corrections baked in from the gate review.\n\n**Most dangerous gotchas (executor must internalize):**\n1. The plan TEXT says `cancelRender()` on unmount \u2014 this is WRONG. The gate warnings override: use `continueRender(handle)` on unmount. cancelRender throws and aborts the render.\n2. The plan TEXT says `delayRender()` on mount \u2014 ambiguous. The gate clarifies: acquire via `useState(() => delayRender(...))` for synchronous blocking, NOT in useEffect.\n3. The plan TEXT says `interpolate(...)` in useAudioParam \u2014 unnecessary. Use `value * (max - min) + min`.\n4. The plan TEXT says \"dual-value Slider or two number inputs\" \u2014 the Slider is scalar-only. Use two NumberInput controls.\n\n**Execution order:** T1 is the foundation (types). T2+T3+T4 are sequential (provider \u2192 hook \u2192 injection). T5, T6 can run in parallel with T2-T4 since they only depend on T1. T7, T8 depend on T2 (need the provider). T9 is the final validation gate after everything else.\n\n**FFT implementation:** Use a minimal inline radix-2 FFT. At 44.1kHz/30fps, each frame window is ~1470 samples \u2192 use 1024-point FFT (512 unique bins, ~43Hz resolution). If the FFT + analysis code pushes AudioAnalysisProvider past ~300 lines, extract FFT to a utility file like `src/tools/video-editor/compositions/fft.ts`.\n\n**Testing gap acknowledged:** The edge function index.test.ts mocks buildGenerateEffectMessages, so it can't validate templates.ts edits. T9 includes adding a direct templates.ts smoke test to close this gap.",
